## Romantic Underwater Cinematic Scene

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{"scene": {"setting": "Underwater, just beneath the surface", "atmosphere": "Romantic, cinematic, dreamlike, high-drama", "lighting": "Dramatic golden hour sunlight filtering through water, creating caustic patterns, deep shadows, and high contrast", "color_palette": "Deep teal and blue water, vibrant orange dress, golden sunlight, soft pink and white accents"}, "subjects": {"woman": {"pose": "Profile view, looking up towards the man, face close to his, floating effortlessly", "appearance": "Fair skin, long dark brown hair flowing naturally in the water, soft facial features", "attire": "Vibrant orange gown with flowing, translucent ruffles and layers that drift in the current"}, "man": {"pose": "Profile view, leaning down towards the woman, eyes closed, face inches from hers", "appearance": "Dark hair, light facial stubble, sharp jawline, sun caustics reflecting on his skin", "attire": "White button-up dress shirt, unbuttoned at the collar, completely soaked and clinging"}}, "environmental_elements": {"water_surface": "Visible just above their heads, creating a clear, wavy reflection of the couple", "marine_life": "Small, glowing pink and orange fish swimming around the subjects", "particles": "Floating orange and pink flower petals, delicate water bubbles, and shimmering micro-particles suspended in the water", "background": "Faint, out-of-focus underwater structures, coral reefs, and ancient pillars adding depth"}, "cinematography": {"camera_angle": "Eye-level medium shot, vertical composition", "focus": "Sharp focus on the couple, shallow depth of field blurring the distant background", "style": "Epic cinematic film still, anamorphic lens quality, rich textures, 4k resolution, high dynamic range (HDR)"}}
```

```
An impasto palette knife oil painting of a beautiful woman with captivating {argument name="eye color" default="brown"} eyes and {argument name="hair type" default="wavy dark"} hair. The portrait is a vibrant explosion of color, utilizing thick, textured strokes of deep blue, fiery orange, rich red, and shimmering gold that blend seamlessly across her face and flow into her hair. The background is a clean, textured white canvas, making the bold, energetic colors of the figure dynamically pop forward.
```

## Extreme Low Angle Airplane Scene

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high-quality realistic photo, vertical format, cinematic style, high detail, dynamic scene with a strong sense of presence and intense air movement. COMPOSITION AND CAMERA ANGLE: The shot is taken from a very low point extreme low angle the camera is directed upward creating a sense of dominance and scale. In the center of the composition is a boy occupying the lower and middle part of the frame. Above his strictly along the central axis in the upper part of the frame is a huge passenger airplane flying at extremely low altitude. The airplane is seen from below its underside fuselage landing gear and two engines on the sides are visible. The perspective enhances the drama the airplane appears massive and looming directly above the subject. The frame is built so that the girl and the airplane form one vertical line strengthening the visual effect. SCENE AND ATMOSPHERE: An open outdoor space the sky occupies most of the background. The sky is partly cloudy with soft voluminous clouds the lighting is natural daylight. The atmosphere is tense dynamic with elements of action. Around the subject particles of dust small stones and debris are lifted into the air by the powerful airflow from the airplane engines. Turbulence is felt in the air all elements of the scene appear to be in motion. There is slight motion blur on flying particles creating a sense of speed. CHARACTER: A young man with expressive facial features and a calm confident charisma. The face is partially hidden by dark sunglasses with a large frame adding mystery. The skin looks natural with realistic texture. The lips are slightly closed the expression is calm confident and slightly defiant against the chaotic scene. HAIR: Stylish thick dark hair and to the sides by the strong airflow from the airplane. Individual strands lift upward creating a sense of chaotic motion and energy. POSE AND BODY LANGUAGE: The boy is sitting on an elevated surface such as a parapet concrete curb or platform edge. One leg is bent at the knee and pulled up with the foot placed on the surface. The other leg hangs down creating a relaxed asymmetrical pose. One hand rests on the raised knee the other hangs relaxed. The torso is slightly leaned forward shoulders relaxed the pose is confident and effortless showing control. The head is slightly raised the chin slightly up. CLOTHING: Casual style with a slightly rough aesthetic. he is wearing a plaid shirt in red blue white tones, Light blue jeans with ripped elements at the knees slightly loose fit. On his feet sneakers in the style of Converse with a recognizable shape and rubber sole. The clothing reacts to the wind fabric is slightly stretched and moving. LIGHTING: Natural daylight soft diffused by clouds without harsh shadows. Light contrast emphasizes textures of skin fabric and the metal surface of the airplane. Light falls from above and slightly from the front creating soft modeling of the face and body. DETAILS AND TEXTURES: High detail visible denim texture folds of the shirt metallic shine of the airplane engine turbines. Dust particles and small debris in the air have varying sharpness. Hair is detailed down to individual strands. Reflections of the environment and light are visible on the sunglasses. MOOD AND STYLE: Cinematic action with elements of fashion photography. Contrast between the calm confident pose of the subject and the chaotic powerful environment around. Atmosphere of strength independence boldness and visual drama. The aesthetic resembles an advertising poster or a frame from a dynamic film.
```

## Miniature Person Sitting on Watermelon

> 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
On a white tabletop, half a large watermelon is placed upright with the flesh facing up (cut in half from the middle). In the picture, a miniature version of the person (with the original facial features preserved) sits on the huge watermelon, with hands crossed, smiling, and both legs naturally perpendicular to the tabletop without touching it
```

## Four-Panel Lifestyle Collage Portrait

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an image. This image is a four-panel collage featuring a {argument name="subject" default="woman"} in a chic, casual outdoor setting, likely a sidewalk cafe. The overall aesthetic is bright and airy, with a warm, sun-drenched "lifestyle" feel.

The woman has long, wavy brunette with blonde highlights hair styled with a soft, voluminous blowout. Her makeup is natural and dewy, featuring a warm lip color and a friendly, relaxed expression. 

She is wearing a coordinated casual outfit:
* Top: {argument name="top" default="An oversized, boxy T-shirt in a dark yellow. The front features a prominent graphic of two interlocking hearts outlined in red with the word 'Love' printed underneath."}
* Bottoms: {argument name="bottoms" default="Classic high-waisted denim shorts in a medium blue wash."}
* Accessories: Simple gold hoop earrings and necklace and a few delicate rings. 
* Footwear: Minimalist yellow strappy flat sandals. 

The collage shows the subject from various angles: 
* Top Left: A seated medium shot, looking directly at the camera. 
* Top Right: A full-length walking shot holding an iced latte in a clear plastic cup. 
* Bottom Left: A full-length side profile shot, highlighting the oversized fit of the shirt. 
* Bottom Right: A close-up seated shot focusing on her face and the shirt's graphic detail. 

The background consists of a Parisian-style street cafe with wooden slatted chairs, small round tables, and large cream-colored patio umbrellas. Soft dappled sunlight filters through the scene, creating a clean, high-end editorial look.
```

```
She has a pale pink manicure on her fingernails.
Several small, minimalist tattoos are visible: one on her right inner forearm, faint markings on her right fingers, and a tiny heart on her left inner arm.

Pose and Action

She is looking directly at the camera with a playful expression.
In her right hand, she is holding a {argument name="object" default="chocolate-dipped ice cream cone"} wrapped at the base with a white paper napkin.
Her left hand is holding a small, clear plastic tasting spoon, which is playfully resting between her puckered lips.

Setting and Lighting

The background consists of warm, medium-toned wood paneling, suggesting a classic or vintage interior.
To the left side of the frame, an illuminated glass display case, typical of a gelato or ice cream shop, is partially visible.

The lighting is warm and even, casting soft shadows and highlighting the golden tones of the wood and the subject's skin.
```

## Monochromatic Film Noir Male Portrait

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{argument name="subject" default="A male"} in his {argument name="age range" default="late twenties to early thirties"} stands positioned on the right side of the frame, facing the camera with a serious, contemplative expression and an intense, direct gaze beneath relaxed eyebrows and a closed, neutral mouth. He has medium-short dark hair pushed back with natural volume and slight, organic flyaways. He wears a {argument name="clothing" default="dark, double-breasted overcoat over a crisp white shirt collar"}, his arms deliberately clasped at his chest, leaning his weight gently to the left while keeping his shoulders level. His relaxed left hand rests naturally against his right bicep, a metallic watch gleaming on his slightly bent wrist. He leans against a thick, worn, cylindrical painted metal pole in the right foreground, its surface heavily detailed with visible textures, chips, and seams. Behind him, the midground opens up to the rippled surface of a canal, flanked on the left by a weathered, ornate railing featuring vertical balusters and a decorative geometric top rail mounted on a rough, matte gray concrete base. The deep, blurred background reveals a pristine, multi-story classical architectural stone building with rows of grand windows across the water, establishing a profound spatial depth. The scene is rendered entirely in monochromatic black and white, illuminated by moody, dramatic, single-source natural light diffused softly from above. Subtle, carefully placed highlights graze the subject's forehead, the bridge of his nose, his cheekbones, the dial of his metallic watch, and the distant water reflections, while short, soft-edged gray shadows gather gracefully under his jaw, deep within the folds of his dark coat, and underneath his crossed arms, culminating in a sophisticated, slightly melancholic film noir atmosphere. Captured as a tack-sharp digital photograph with medium depth of field inspired by classic European cinema and documentary street portraiture, the image is shot straight on using a 35mm lens at f/2.8 and a shutter speed of 1/250s. The smooth textures are further enhanced by precise post-processing that includes a medium contrast curve, a slight lift in the black point for a faded cinematic look, deliberate dodging on the subject's face to cement the visual hierarchy, and an application of simulated 35mm film grain, all elegantly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

```
Subject and Composition
Focal Point: A young man is lying asleep in the middle of a wide expanse of {argument name="ground cover" default="green grass"}. He poses with both hands open to the side while lying on his back, facing the camera above. His hair is slightly messy from the wind.

Clothes:
{argument name="jacket" default="Dark black denim trucker jacket"} with a built in light gray cotton hoodie, creating a layered streetwear look.
Black denim pants with a very wide baggy fit from hips to ankles, giving a loose and relaxed silhouette.
Classic low top Vans Authentic sneakers.
Color: Black canvas with white shoelaces and white metal eyelets, paired with slightly visible white socks.

Perspective:
The photo is taken perfectly perpendicular from above at a 90 degree angle, creating a cinematic sense of scale between the young man and the surrounding nature. The contrast between the vibrant green grass and the deep blue ocean creates a dramatic and peaceful atmosphere.
```

## Candid Handheld iPhone Style Mall Photography

> Minimal yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Casual candid iPhone style photograph of a young person sitting comfortably inside a busy {argument name="location" default="Brazilian shopping mall food court"} during the afternoon, holding a {argument name="drink" default="Starbucks cup"} in one hand and a dessert tray in the other, creating a relaxed everyday lifestyle atmosphere. The person is dressed in {argument name="clothing" default="simple casual clothing like an oversized hoodie, loose jeans, everyday sneakers"}, and minimal accessories, looking natural instead of overly styled. Their posture feels authentic and unposed, like someone casually taking a quick break between shopping or meeting friends. Expression should feel calm, soft, and spontaneous with natural body language.

The food court environment should feel lively and realistic with multiple people sitting at tables in the background, families eating, teenagers talking, workers carrying trays, and small details that make the mall feel genuinely active and naturally crowded. Include recognizable Brazilian mall architecture and atmosphere with modern seating arrangements, polished floors, colorful food signs, and mixed fast food restaurants around the area. Trays, napkins, partially eaten meals, shopping bags, and drink cups scattered naturally across nearby tables should help create a believable environment.

Camera perspective should feel like an authentic handheld iPhone photo taken casually by a friend at eye level, with slightly imperfect composition and mildly messy framing. Avoid cinematic perfection. Keep the framing natural with a little asymmetry and casual cropping, like a real spontaneous social media photo. Lighting should combine warm indoor mall lighting with cooler ambient tones coming from storefronts and ceiling lights, creating realistic mixed color temperatures. Include soft natural shadows, subtle reflections on tables, and slight overexposure in some highlights to mimic smartphone photography realism.

Add realistic smartphone image qualities like slight grain, tiny motion softness, mild blur in background movement, realistic skin texture, and balanced but imperfect exposure. Colors should appear true to life rather than heavily filtered, with natural tones and believable contrast. The Starbucks cup and dessert should feel casually integrated into the moment instead of staged product placement. Overall mood should capture an ordinary but aesthetically pleasing slice of daily life in a Brazilian mall food court, emphasizing authenticity, spontaneity, and relatable modern lifestyle energy.
```

## Candid Grocery Store Aisle Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: A hyper-photorealistic candid grocery store photo of the same subject in the attached image.

Scene:
Inside a {argument name="location" default="grocery store beverage aisle"}, the woman is reaching for a drink placed on the very top refrigerator shelf. She cannot quite reach it, so she is slightly tiptoeing, stretching her arm upward with effort. The moment feels candid, awkward, and realistic, like the camera caught her mid-reach.

Camera angle / lens perspective logic:
Shot from a high-level angle, as if the camera is placed near the top edge of the refrigerator shelf looking downward at the woman. Use a wide smartphone lens perspective, around 24–28mm equivalent. The hand closest to the camera appears noticeably larger due to perspective distortion, while her face and body appear smaller below.

Subject-to-camera distance:
Her reaching hand is very close to the camera, around 20–30 cm away. Her face is about 1.2 meters below the camera. The drinks on the top shelf are partially visible near the camera edge.

Foreground obstruction ratio:
The drinks she is reaching for occupy 18% of the upper foreground frame. Her enlarged reaching hand occupies 22% of the center-upper frame. A refrigerator shelf edge cuts across 8% of the top frame.

Eye-line angle:
Her eyes look upward toward the drink, about 35 degrees above her natural eye line. She is not looking at the camera. Her brows are slightly raised, lips slightly parted from effort.

Motion vector path:
Her arm stretches diagonally upward from lower-right to upper-left. Fingers extend toward the drink cap. Her body weight shifts onto the balls of her feet, knees slightly straightened, shoulder lifted.

Frame occupancy:
Her enlarged hand is the dominant foreground element. Her face occupies around 14% of the frame height. Her full upper body occupies around 45% of the frame height. Keep the top-shelf drinks visibly close to the lens.

Pose:
One arm fully extended upward, fingers almost touching the bottle. Other arm slightly bent near her side for balance. She is standing on tiptoe, heels slightly lifted, torso stretched long, chin tilted upward.

Outfit:
{argument name="outfit" default="Casual grocery outfit: fitted white baby tee, loose light-wash straight-leg jeans, simple sneakers, thin bracelet on reaching wrist, small shoulder bag hanging from one shoulder"}.

Hair:
Loose natural hair falling backward slightly because her head is tilted up, a few strands near her cheek and neck, realistic flyaways.

Makeup:
Natural everyday makeup, dewy skin, soft blush, realistic pores, slight under-eye texture, natural brows, muted pink lips.

Background:
Bright grocery store beverage refrigerator aisle with rows of bottled drinks, soda cans, juice bottles, price tags, cool white refrigerator lighting, metal shelf rails, glass door reflections, slightly cluttered real-store arrangement.

Camera / realism:
Shot on {argument name="camera" default="iPhone 15 Pro 0.5x or 1x wide lens"}, crisp smartphone detail, cool refrigerator lighting, normal white balance, slight wide-a
```

```
Overlay the image with vibrant, hand-drawn doodle effects: white drawn outlines around the subject, playful scribbles, stars, sparkles, electric sparks, and floating white and black handwritten slogans like 'forever young', 'krl', 'night', and 'gen z vibes'. The style should seamlessly blend hyper-realistic photography with colorful, flat cartoon illustrations. Keeping face and all shape person unchanged.
```

## iPhone Style Joyful Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 3:4.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, specifically an iPhone 15 Pro, characterized by its digital clarity and shot on a modern high-end smartphone camera with a wide-angle lens at ISO 50 and f/1.8. The subject is a young adult woman captured from a low-angle perspective, leaning her torso forward and angled 15 degrees to the left. Her pose is a study in human asymmetry: her left arm is extended directly toward the lens in a candid, reaching gesture with relaxed, softly curved fingers, while her right arm hangs back, creating a natural weight shift that causes her right shoulder to drop lower than the left. Her {argument name="hair color" default="dark"} hair is styled with wispy bottleneck bangs and two long, symmetrical braids resting on her chest, with fine flyaway strands caught in a soft breeze. Her expression is one of radiant joy, a beaming smile that causes a slight nose scrunch and a genuine "eye smile" where her eyes are half-lidded with micro-tightening at the outer corners and a glistening inner corner shine. Her brows are relaxed and slightly raised in spontaneous delight. Her skin exhibits a healthy radiance and a distinct rosy warmth, featuring visible pores and a clear texture with soft highlights on the bridge and tip of her nose. A subtle pinkish-mauve gloss covers her lips with a subtle shimmer, and a high-placed pink blush accents her cheekbones. She is dressed in a {argument name="clothing" default="light blue ribbed cotton camisole crop top"} under an {argument name="outerwear" default="oversized, cobalt blue chunky-knit cardigan"} decorated with large, white intarsia cloud patterns; the heavy knit texture is microscopically detailed where it slips off her shoulders. She wears high-waisted, distressed indigo denim shorts with intricate fraying and white thread tassels at the hem. The entire scene is rendered in incredibly sharp focus against a vast, saturated azure sky, illuminated by bright, natural overhead sunlight that creates a subtle lens flare and gentle shadows under the jawline. Digital texture is paramount, revealing the crisp fabric details and the soft glow of the skin, devoid of film grain but with a hint of subtle digital noise, maintaining a natural, minimal contrast look.
```

## Apple Orchard 4-Panel Collage Prompt

> Retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high quality realistic photo using the face from the reference without any changes or distortions. Vertical photo. A four-panel collage made of four separate frames combined into one composition, each frame showing the same girl in an {argument name="location" default="apple orchard"} on green grass scattered with many ripe apples. The overall style is consistent across all images: warm natural lighting, soft sunlight passing through tree leaves, creating a dappled pattern of light and shadow on the grass and on the girl’s body. The background in all frames is an orchard with trees extending into the depth, with slight blur emphasizing the main subject.
In the top left frame, the girl is lying on her back on the grass, her body positioned diagonally. One hand rests on her stomach, the other is raised holding a slice of fruit near her face. Her head is slightly tilted back, her expression relaxed with a soft smile, eyes either gently closed or looking upward. Apples are scattered around her, creating rich red accents against the green grass.

In the top right frame, the girl is in a pose on her hands and knees, her torso slightly leaning forward. One hand supports her on the ground while the other holds an apple near her lips, suggesting a moment just before a bite. Her gaze is directed to the side, not into the camera. The pose is dynamic yet soft and natural. The surrounding space is filled with scattered apples extending into the depth of the frame.

In the bottom left frame, the girl is sitting on the grass with her knees pulled close to her chest and her arms wrapped around them. A small woven basket stands nearby. She is slightly leaning forward, her gaze directed downward or to the side, with a warm gentle smile. Apples are scattered around her, forming a dense pattern on the grass.

In the bottom right frame, the girl is kneeling with an upright torso and slightly open shoulders. Her arms are crossed or gently wrapped around herself, creating an elegant silhouette line. Her head is turned to the side, her gaze directed into the distance. The pose is calm, graceful, and focused on body lines.

In all frames she wears the same {argument name="outfit" default="fitted long-sleeve dress"} with a mini length that accentuates her figure. The look is completed with light high socks or stockings. A {argument name="accessory" default="headscarf"} is tied over her hair, securing it and adding a vintage touch. Her hair is long, flowing over her shoulders or resting on the grass depending on the pose.

The color palette is rich, with strong contrast between green grass and red apples, while warm sunlight adds a golden tone to the scene. Depth of field is moderate: the girl is in sharp focus while the background is softly blurred. The overall atmosphere is summery, cozy, slightly playful, with natural outdoor photography aesthetics and a focus on harmony between the subject and nature.
2:3ar
```

## 90s Japanese Indie Music Store Aesthetic

> Retro yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
subject is captured in a candid, natural moment inside a {argument name="location" default="cluttered Japanese 1990s indie music store"} during daytime. Tight close-up shot (head and shoulders), dynamic canted composition with a slightly low and off-center angle that feels spontaneous and intimate.
She leans casually near the large floor-to-ceiling window with subtle teal tint overlooking a busy downtown city area, wearing a {argument name="accessory" default="vintage silver Sony Walkman with classic yellow foam headphones"}, the cord gently draped across her chest. Her outfit is random 1990s Japanese indie thrift energy: oversized faded gray graphic band t-shirt layered under an open navy corduroy shirt. Her shoulder-length layered hair is messy with choppy bangs, slightly tousled.
Her expression is quiet and introspective, with a subtle peeking gaze directed straight toward the viewer — heavy-lidded eyes that feel both distant and quietly engaging, caught in a fleeting natural moment.
All lighting originates from the large teal-tinted window as the single main source: strong backlight, soft lens flare, and delicate light leaks/semburat cahaya spilling warmly across her hair, shoulders, and side of the face. The window creates dramatic uneven illumination with high contrast. A warm, dim indoor lighting (soft reddish-amber glow) from inside the store gently fills the shadows, blending beautifully with the cooler teal backlight from the window and producing rich chiaroscuro effect — deep moody shadows on one side of her face and body, while the other side is beautifully rim-lit by the window.
Extremely shallow depth of field creates dreamy, creamy bokeh. In the soft-focus foreground, blurred edges of vinyl records and a dangling cassette tape produce beautiful bokeh orbs and layered visual depth. Color grading is a rich {argument name="film style" default="warm vintage Kodak 1990s aesthetic"} perfectly blended with teal-green undertones — golden-amber highlights mixed with cool teal shadows and midtones from the window, subtle magenta accents, visible cinematic film grain, and a very soft, delicate Promist halation that gently diffuses only the brightest edges. The overall mood is melancholic yet quietly intimate, photorealistic yet deeply dreamlike, evoking the golden era of 90s Japanese indie music culture with strong analog soul and dramatic chiaroscuro lighting.
```

```
Skin: Soft, fresh, and rosy natural skin with visible pores. Genuine, innocent joy in her expression.

Environment & Atmosphere
Setting: A hidden, sun-drenched glade in a magical, ancient forest.
Background: Massive ancient tree trunks covered in glowing vines. A carpet of vibrant, oversized glowing wildflowers (bokeh effect). Floating dandelion seeds and magical golden spores drift heavily in the air.
Lighting: Breathtaking, volumetric golden-hour god rays piercing through the thick forest canopy, acting as a spotlight directly on her. The lighting is soft, warm, and deeply romantic, creating a glowing halo effect around her.

Camera & Capture
Camera: Hasselblad H6D-100c. Lens: 85mm f/1.2. Aperture: f/1.4 (for extreme creamy bokeh, melting the deer and forest into a dreamy background). ISO: 200. Shutter: 1/500. Rich, warm, fairy-tale color grading.
```

## 4K Wallpaper Prompt of a Baby Tiger Cub using Gemini Nano Banana Pro

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"A high-resolution photograph of a baby tiger cub, viewed head-on with an intense, curious gaze, peeking over a textured, dark green surface that matches the background. The cub's large brown eyes, full of wonder and innocence, are a prominent feature, drawing the viewer's focus. Its rounded ears are perked up, catching the light and emphasizing its attentive expression. The cub's front paws, with visible pads and small claws, rest on the ledge, positioned symmetrically on either side of its face. Its striking orange-gold fur is detailed with dark, well-defined black stripes, forming distinct patterns on its forehead, cheeks, and around its eyes. Soft, white fur accents are on its cheeks, inner ears, and chest. The cub's long white whiskers spread out prominently from its muzzle. The lighting is soft and even, highlighting the textures of the cub's dense fur, the shine in its eyes, and the pebbled texture of the green surface it is peeking over. The focus is critically sharp on the cub's eyes and face, capturing fine details in its fur, whiskers, and eye structure. The depth of field is shallow, rendering the rich green background as a smooth, uniform bokeh, free of any distracting details, to isolate the subject. The style is that of a professional wildlife portrait, with a natural yet intimate feel, as if a momentary glimpse into the cub's world. The composition is balanced and tight on the cub's head and paws, filling the lower portion of the frame."
```

## Photo Booth Strip Narrative Prompt for Nano Banana 2

> Minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
CRITICAL: Preserve both people's facial features exactly as in the reference photos. Both must be clearly recognizable.

Create two vertical photo booth strips side by side on a dark red textured fabric background. Each strip has 4 square black-and-white frames stacked vertically with white borders.

The story: he gradually leaves HIS strip (right) and appears in HER strip (left).

LEFT STRIP:
Frame 1: woman ALONE, smiling at camera
Frame 2: couple TOGETHER — he has appeared next to her, they look at each other laughing
Frame 3: couple kissing, eyes closed
Frame 4: couple embracing, both smiling at camera

RIGHT STRIP:
Frame 1: man ALONE, smiling at camera
Frame 2: man ALONE, looking sideways toward her, as if about to leave his frame
Frame 3: the frame is almost empty — only a faint shadow or slight blur where he was, he has left
Frame 4: empty frame — just the photo booth background, no person, he is gone completely

All frames black-and-white, high contrast, slight film grain. Direct front flash, flat photo booth lighting. Both wear simple dark clothing. Tight framing — head and shoulders. Strips are separate, never merging.
```

```
“Using the ATTACHED REFERENCE IMAGE as the identity source, create an ultra-detailed Baroque-style portrait that preserves the subject’s exact facial identity, bone structure, proportions, and skin texture with absolute fidelity. The subject is positioned in a classical aristocratic portrait composition, facing slightly off-center with a calm yet intense expression, evoking psychological depth and authority. Lighting: dramatic chiaroscuro with strong directional key light from one side, deep shadow falloff, and subtle Rembrandt triangle lighting on the face. Background is dark, rich, and painterly, with soft gradients and minimal distractions. Wardrobe: opulent 17th-century aristocratic attire with luxurious fabrics such as velvet, silk, and brocade, featuring intricate embroidery, lace details, and gold accents. Materials must show realistic texture, weight, and light interaction. Rendering style: oil painting realism with visible brushwork, soft blending, micro-texture, and subtle canvas grain. Inspired by classical Baroque masters like Rembrandt. Camera & optics: medium close-up portrait, 50mm lens feel, shallow depth of field (f/1.8), sharp focus on eyes, smooth falloff. Color grading: warm golden tones, deep shadows, rich contrast, cinematic dynamic range. Focus on depth, realism, material fidelity, and emotional intensity. Ultra-high detail, museum-grade finish, rich painterly realism.”
```

```
A woman jumping on a puddle on a rainy night, full-body composition, 8-heads tall model figure. Wet short bob hair flowing, one arm raised high and one leg extended backward in that moment. ## Split-Style Portrait: Photo vs. Digital Illustration
```

> Minimal, fütüristik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a split-style portrait in ultra-detailed 8K where the subject is vertically divided into two contrasting halves.
Left Half: Realistic photo, natural lighting, enhanced sharpness, authentic textures. Add thin neon or pastel doodle outlines for a subtle creative touch.
Right Half: Convert the same subject into a clean digital illustration (chibi or semi-realistic style), keeping identical pose and expression. Use smooth lineart, soft shading, and pleasing pastel tones. Include small floating decorative stickers and cute elements.
Important: The background must remain 100% identical and unedited across both halves.
Divider: Vertical torn/glitch transition line.
Style: Clean, modern, aesthetic, Instagram-ready
Quality: 8K, ultra-detailed, --ar 3:4
```

## Awkward Stacked NFT Family Portrait

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A deliberately awkward and playful studio family portrait featuring uploaded characters. The composition must recreate the feeling of an unnatural, slightly uncomfortable human stack — similar to classic awkward family portraits where people are leaning, sitting on each other, or balancing in strange but stable ways. --- 🔒 IDENTITY LOCK (CRITICAL) Each character must strictly preserve: • original AKCB proportions (large head, short limbs) • eye shape, spacing, stylization • balaclava fully covering nose and mouth (never visible) • original materials, textures, and colors All characters must remain equal in scale. No size drift. No realism increase. --- 🧱 STRUCTURE SYSTEM — “AWKWARD STACK LOGIC” (CORE) The group must form a **stacked or interlocked structure** using their bodies. Use combinations like: • one character acting as a base (lying or crouched) • one sitting sideways on another’s back or legs • one perched or kneeling on top • one leaning awkwardly across others for balance • limbs used as support points (shoulders, knees, backs) The pose should feel: • slightly uncomfortable • physically questionable but still believable • intentionally staged and awkward Avoid clean posing. Avoid symmetry. --- ⚖️ BALANCE RULE (IMPORTANT) Even though the pose is silly: • the structure must feel physically possible • weight distribution must make sense • characters must visibly support each other No floating. No broken physics. --- 😐 EXPRESSION & BODY LANGUAGE • neutral or mildly serious expressions (contrast with silly pose) • relaxed faces, awkward bodies • no exaggerated cartoon acting --- 📸 STUDIO STYLE • classic portrait studio background (gray / muted color / soft gradient) • soft even lighting • subtle shadows grounding each character • clean, slightly outdated family photo aesthetic --- 🎯 COMPOSITION • full body of all characters visible • clear stacking hierarchy (bottom → middle → top) • no messy overlaps hiding faces --- 🚫 NEGATIVE CONSTRAINTS • no extra limbs • no merging of characters • no pose duplication • no symmetry • no action poses (this is static portrait energy) • no extreme exaggeration --- ✨ VARIATION ENGINE Each generation must create a DIFFERENT awkward stacked pose using the same logic. Examples of variation: • pyramid stack • sideways bench stack • diagonal leaning tower • tangled sitting cluster • “one person is clearly struggling” composition
ar 1:1
```

The hair is styled in long, voluminous dark waves swept over one shoulder and adorned with a white floral accessory. ## Impossible Reflection - Parallel Reality Puddle

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a street scene where a large rain puddle on a cobblestone street shows a COMPLETELY DIFFERENT world in its reflection. The real world and reflected world are different realities.

REAL WORLD (top half):
- A quiet modern European cobblestone street on a grey overcast day
- A person in a {argument name="real world coat color" default="grey"} coat walking with an umbrella
- Modern buildings, parked cars, a bicycle
- Bare winter trees
- Muted, desaturated, cold — typical rainy Tuesday afternoon

PUDDLE REFLECTION (bottom half — inverted):
- Instead of reflecting the real world above, the puddle shows a DIFFERENT version:
- The same street but in a parallel fantasy universe: the buildings are covered in lush tropical vegetation, the trees are in full cherry blossom bloom
- The grey-coated person's reflection shows them in flowing colorful robes
- The parked car's reflection is a horse-drawn carriage
- The sky in the reflection is a stunning sunset with aurora borealis
- Flying whales visible in the reflected sky
- The cobblestones in the reflection are made of gold

TECHNICAL:
- The puddle is large enough to fill the bottom 45% of the frame
- The real world fills the top 55%
- The reflection is perfectly inverted (as puddle reflections are) but shows this alternate reality
- Some ripples in the puddle distort the fantasy world slightly — proving it's a water surface
- The edges where puddle meets cobblestone show the transition between worlds
- Rain drops hitting the puddle surface create small circular ripples that briefly distort the fantasy world

STYLE: The real world is photorealistic, muted, cold. The reflected world is vivid, warm, magical — the contrast between the two realities is the entire point. Shot at low angle (camera nearly on the ground) to maximize the puddle reflection. Slightly wide angle (24mm equivalent). 8K detail in both worlds.
```

## Cinematic Portrait in Sage-Green Grass Field

```
Create a cinematic editorial-style portrait in a 3:4 aspect ratio, with an atmospheric mood. Use the attached image as the exact reference for the subject’s face and identity, ensuring a perfect match. The subject (male or female) stands alone in the middle of a vast sage-green grass field, swept by strong winds. Tall grass surrounds the subject entirely, moving like ocean waves, partially obscuring the body and creating a sense of motion, depth, and isolation. The subject is wearing the exact same outfit as in the reference image.

The hair appears slightly messy, naturally blown by the wind. The head is tilted slightly downward, with the gaze directed toward the ground, conveying a quiet, introspective, and melancholic mood. The facial expression is neutral, distant, and emotionally controlled. Compose the frame with the subject slightly off-center, using a slightly elevated camera angle. There should be no visible horizon only layers of grass filling the frame. Use muted natural tones such as sage green, deep olive green, and soft whites.

Shoot in vertical 3:4 format using a telephoto lens with shallow depth of field. Frame the image in a cinematic style with a film photography aesthetic, including noticeable grain, soft edges, atmospheric lighting, and motion blur in the grass caused by wind. The overall tone should feel poetic, calm, and introspective, with high detail while maintaining a natural look. Magazine-cover quality, with no text or watermarks.
```

## Cinematic Festival Photo of Millie Bobby Brown

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"Create a highly detailed, cinematic, photorealistic lifestyle photograph of {argument name="subject" default="Millie Bobby Brown"} at an outdoor festival during early evening. She has long, slightly wavy hair and a playful expression, smiling with her teeth visible while looking back over her right shoulder directly at the camera. Her makeup is light and natural. She is wearing sunglasses resting on top of her head, large teal-colored drop-shaped earrings, and a bright yellow festival wristband on her right wrist.

She has a slim, fit body and is dressed in a light greenish-gray cropped short-sleeve t-shirt with a faded print on the front, paired with light blue ripped denim cut-off shorts. On her feet, she wears classic black high-top canvas sneakers with white laces, a white toe cap, and white soles.

Her pose is dynamic and confident: she is standing with her back facing the camera, her torso twisted as she looks over her right shoulder. Her right arm is slightly raised with a relaxed hand near her waist, while her left arm hangs naturally. Her weight is mostly on her extended left leg, with her right leg slightly bent, creating a casual and energetic posture.

The setting is a lively outdoor festival, resembling an amusement park. In the background, there is a large Ferris wheel illuminated with neon pink and white lights along its spokes and rim. Blurred figures of people walking can be seen in the background, and the ground appears dark, like a temporary paved surface.

The lighting combines soft dusk light with strong, cool artificial ambient light coming from the Ferris wheel. The neon lights create a glowing rim light around her hair, shoulders, and silhouette, while a soft front fill light clearly illuminates her face and body.

The overall mood is vibrant, youthful, energetic, and cinematic, capturing the lively atmosphere of a summer festival. The color palette emphasizes neon pink, bright white, light denim blue, greenish-gray, black, and soft pink tones.

The image should be captured in a portrait orientation (9:16), using a slightly low camera angle and a tight framing (medium close-up or American shot), with the subject occupying almost the entire frame. Use a telephoto lens between 85mm and 135mm to compress perspective and emphasize the subject, with a shallow depth of field to keep her extremely sharp while the background remains softly blurred with bokeh. The final result should be highly detailed, with sharp focus on the subject, vibrant and contrasting colors, professional lighting, and 8K resolution quality."
```

## Hyperrealistic Anime Spotlight Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Generate a hyperrealistic realistic-anime portrait of a female character standing in a completely black background.
Lighting: use a **narrow beam spotlight** focused only on the center of the face. 
The edges of the light must be sharp and dramatic. 
All areas outside the spotlight should fall quickly into deep darkness 
(high falloff shadow), almost blending into the black background. 
Not soft lighting.
Hair: long dark hair with some strands falling over the face. The lower parts of the hair should fade into the shadows.
Pose: one hand raised gently to the lips in a shy, hesitant gesture. 
Eyes looking directly at the camera with a mysterious mood.
Clothing: black long-sleeve knit sweater; 
the sweater and body should mostly disappear into the darkness with minimal detail.
Overall tone: dark, moody, dramatic, mysterious. 
High-contrast only in the lit portion of the face. 
Everything outside the spotlight should be nearly invisible.
```

## Cinematic Editorial Portrait in Wind-Swept Grass Fields

> Sinematik, minimal manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a 3:4 cinematic, atmospheric editorial portrait using the attached photo as the exact face and identity reference. A solitary man/woman stands alone in the middle of vast, wind-swept sage green grass fields. Tall grass surrounds the subject completely, flowing in strong currents like ocean waves, partially obscuring the body and creating motion,depth, and solitude. The subject wears a simple, loose white shirt that softly contrasts with the rich green landscape.
Dark hair is slightly messy, with natural strands moving in the wind. The head is gently tilted downward, eyes cast toward the ground, conveying a quiet, introspective, melancholic mood. Expression is calm, distant, and emotionally restrained. Compose the frame slightly off-center, from a subtle elevated angle. No visible horizon, the frame is fully immersed in grass texture. Use a muted natural palette of sage green, deep olive, and soft off-white. Shoot vertical 3:4, telephoto perspective, shallow depth of field, cinematic framing. Film photography aesthetic with visible grain, soft edge falloff, atmospheric light, wind-driven motion blur in the grass. Quiet, poetic storytelling; ultra-detailed yet natural, magazine-cover editorial quality. No text, no watermark.
```

## Blonde Girl Walking Down a Tree-Lined Street Video Prompt

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Against a backdrop of a tree-lined street with sunlight filtering through the leaves and a clear blue sky, a blonde girl wearing a blue checked uniform and black platform shoes walks lightly. She stops and looks back over her shoulder with a shy smile, turns while holding the hem of her skirt, and looks up at the sky while hugging her black school bag to her chest. Finally, she waves at the camera with a beaming smile and runs off into the light. A video characterized by a pop and refreshing atmosphere and dynamic camera work.
```

## iPhone Photo Dump Concert Collage

> Retro, stüdyo ışıklı manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A vertical photo collage in mobile phone quality showing three panels: a wide landscape shot at top, a portrait in the middle, and a macro detail at bottom. The images follow a VSCO-style photo dump aesthetic with warm, vibrant concert-style color grading shot on a simulated iPhone 17 Pro Max with varied lenses (ultrawide, wide, telephoto) at f/1.8 aperture. The lighting comes from dynamic concert sources including strobe lights and phone glow, creating shimmering highlights on skin and deep contrasty shadows. The scene takes place in a massive music arena at night with stadium floor access and stage view, featuring a video wall and silhouetted crowd hands. The subject is a 21-year-old athletic Nordic influencer with blonde hair and blue eyes, wearing black Wonderbra and V-Thong with black high heels. Her skin has a sun-kissed glow with visible subsurface scattering from stage heat, and she has realistic biological details like peach fuzz on cheeks and visible iris detail. The middle panel shows her winking with a peace sign over one eye, while the bottom panel captures her walking in heels. The composition uses a first-person perspective with rule of thirds in each panel and asymmetrical balance. The color is selectively enhanced on confetti with magenta and cyan pops. The overall effect should feel like an authentic night out at a concert, avoiding studio lighting, vintage film grain, or overly polished looks.
```

## Consistent Identity Burst Photo Simulation

```
Use the uploaded reference photo as the only facial identity reference.
The same exact face must appear in all three frames.
Do not change identity, do not beautify, do not stylize, do not reinterpret the face.
Facial identity must stay perfectly consistent across all frames.

Ultra-realistic vertical photo divided into three equal stacked frames inside one single image.
All three frames show the same man, same clothes, same location, captured seconds apart like a casual smartphone burst photo.
Natural imperfections allowed. Slight motion blur allowed. Identity must stay identical.

Location:
Outdoor sidewalk in daylight.
White plaster wall with Bougainvillea glabra climbing plants.
Natural urban background, realistic, not staged.

Outfit (locked):
Pastel yellow loose button-down shirt, top buttons open, sleeves rolled to forearms, untucked.
Off-white straight fit trousers.
Metal wristwatch.
No sunglasses.

Hair & face (locked):
Medium length wavy messy hair.
Natural skin texture, visible pores.
No beauty filter, no smoothing.

TOP FRAME:
Back angled shot, turning away.
Hand running through back of hair near neck.
Face mostly hidden.
Slight motion blur on hand and hair.
Casual, unposed, Gen-Z energy.

MIDDLE FRAME:
Close-up of upper face.
Hair falling across forehead covering one eye.
Eyes half visible, looking sideways/down.
Soft focus, slight motion softness.
Feels accidental, intimate.

BOTTOM FRAME:
Mid shot.
Leaning casually against wall.
One hand near pocket or adjusting cuff.
Looking away from camera.
Relaxed, natural posture.

Lighting:
Natural daylight, soft shadows, realistic phone camera look.
Not cinematic, not studio, not HDR.

Style:
Ultra realistic
smartphone photo
burst shot
imperfect framing
human, casual, real
identity locked
same person all frames
```

## Hyper-Realistic Smartphone Photo of Woman on Lounger

> Fotogerçekçi, retro manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 3:4.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, specifically an iPhone 15 Pro, characterized by its digital clarity, shot at ISO 50, f/1.8. A young woman reclines on a weathered wooden lounger, her torso angled 45 degrees with a natural weight shift and one hip subtly raised. Her head tilts, resting on her right hand while her left arm is bent sharply behind her head in a mid-moment pose. She wears a voluminous 90s-style blowout with soft mermaid waves. Her expression is serene yet intrigued, featuring a micro-smirk and a smoldering gaze with one brow raised slightly. Her skin shows a healthy radiance and glass finish with visible pores. Forensic makeup includes soft winged liner, champagne shimmer, and a blurred coral lip stain. She is dressed in a pale yellow modest two-piece swimwear with ruffled off-the-shoulder sleeves and a matching sheer crinkle-fabric sarong knotted at the hip. The scene is illuminated by soft, diffused daylight, rendered in sharp focus from the wooden pier textures to the distant mountain ridges and turquoise sea, emphasizing digital texture and crisp fabric details without any bokeh.
```

## Sadie Sink Photoshoot in a Modern Apartment

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-angle, close-up shot of a young woman with vibrant red hair and light freckles lounging on a grey plush sectional sofa. She is lying on her stomach, propped up on her elbows, looking directly into the lens with a calm expression. She wears a simple white ribbed tank top and blue denim jeans. Her hair is slightly damp, with a single wet curl draped over her forehead. In the soft-focus background, a modern open-concept apartment is visible, featuring dark wood kitchen cabinetry, a distinctive bright red retro-style refrigerator, and warm track lighting. Her bare feet are playfully kicked up in the air behind her.

---

A medium-wide shot capturing the woman from a slightly different angle as she rests on the grey sofa. She is positioned horizontally across the frame, lying on her chest with her legs crossed at the ankles and lifted upward. Her face is turned toward the camera with a subtle, relaxed smile, highlighting her rosy cheeks and clear blue eyes. The lighting is warm and domestic, casting soft shadows across the living area. The background remains consistent, showing the kitchen area and a hallway with a decorative mural on the wall, creating an intimate, "at-home" atmosphere.

---

A front-facing perspective of the woman propping herself up on the sofa with both hands planted firmly on the cushions. She is leaning forward toward the camera, her red hair appearing wet or styled with product, pulled back away from her face. She wears the same white tank top and blue jeans, with her freckled arms and shoulders prominent in the foreground. Her legs are still bent at the knees in the background. The scene is illuminated by the overhead warm lights of the apartment, emphasizing the contrast between the dark kitchen units and the pops of color like the red fridge.
```

Her hair is dirty blonde with warm undertones, long waist-length with loose beach waves, styled naturally down. She has tanned bronzed skin with golden undertones and visible musculature, satin-to-oily finish, with highlight zones on collarbone, shoulders, abdominal definition, and thighs.
